# Gig: Going-away Party

Journal: `quests/street_stories/santo_domingo/rancho_coronado/sts_std_rcr_03` · `StreetStory` · hash `302638271`.

Journal premise: Gig type: SOS: Merc Needed\nObjective: Rescue Flavio dos Santos\nLocation: Mallagra St.\nDetails:\n\n6th Street got shaken up with an internal coup, the boss took a bullet in the back of the head. The guy who filled his shoes was the same who pulled the trigger – they call him Gunner.  The new bossman has unleashed a purge to rid the gang of all the people loyal to the last chief. My client, Flavio, is next in line to get shot and he'd very much like to skip town.\n\nAlready struck a deal with the nomads who agreed to ship him to the other coast. All you have to do is escort Flavio to the place their guide will pick him up. Little problem to note: there's already a price on our friend's head, so he's gotta watch it.\n\nSending you coords of where Flavio's holed up. You need to get there, load him into your ride, and deliver to the nomads ASAP. Keep your iron at the ready and don't forget to always check your mirrors. 6th St gave him a death sentence so any tails on his ass are likely to be hot.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 122 exact journal-linked nodes in 5 resources; 11 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 9 content candidate, 2 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

703 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 68 distinct direct resource identities; 41 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.
